```
332	        mode='mid',
333	    )
334	
335	    # 3. Generate
336	    inputs = [item['question'] for item in dataset]
337	    print("Generating responses...")
338	    start_time = time.time()
339	    outputs = model.generate(inputs, max_out_len=65536)
340	    end_time = time.time()
341	    print(f"\nGeneration completed in {end_time - start_time:.2f} seconds")
342	
343	    # 4. Score & Save
344	    print("\n--- Evaluation Results ---")
345	    correct_count = 0
346	    results_to_save = []
347	    tmp_output_file = os.path.join(output_dir, "_tmp_prediction.jsonl")
348	    total_input_tokens = 0
349	    total_output_tokens = 0
350	
351	    mcq_tasks = ['mcq']
352	    long_context_tasks = [
353	        'niah', 'cwe', 'fwe', 'qa', 'lcx'
354	    ]
355	
356	    for i, item in enumerate(dataset):
357	        task = item.get('task', 'unknown')
358	        pred = outputs[i]
359	        gold = item.get('gold')
360	
361	        in_len = model.get_token_len(inputs[i])
362	        out_len = model.get_token_len(pred)
363	        total_input_tokens += in_len
364	        total_output_tokens += out_len
365	
366	        score = 0
367	        extracted = None
368	
369	        if task in mcq_tasks:
370	            score, extracted = score_mcq(pred, gold)
371	        elif task in long_context_tasks:
372	            score = score_exact_match(pred, gold, task)
373	        else:
374	            if isinstance(gold, str) and gold.lower() in pred.lower():
375	                score = 1
376	
377	        correct_count += score
378	
379	        results_to_save.append({
380	            "index": i,
381	            "task": task,
382	            "question": item['question'],
383	            "gold": gold,
384	            "prediction": pred,
385	            "score": score,
386	            "extracted": extracted,
387	            "input_tokens": in_len,
388	            "output_tokens": out_len,
389	        })
390	
391	        if args.verbose:
392	            print(f"\n[Sample {i+1}] Task: {task}")
393	            print(f"Gold: {gold}, Extracted: {extracted}, Score: {score}")
394	            print(f"Tokens: In={in_len}, Out={out_len}")
395	
396	    avg_score = (correct_count / len(dataset)) * 100 if dataset else 0
397	    duration = end_time - start_time
398	    tps = total_output_tokens / duration if duration > 0 else 0
399	
400	    print(f"\nAverage Score: {avg_score:.2f}%")
401	    print(f"Total Duration: {duration:.2f} s")
402	    print(f"Total Tokens: In={total_input_tokens}, Out={total_output_tokens}")
403	    if len(dataset) > 0:
404	        print(f"Average Tokens/Sample: In={total_input_tokens/len(dataset):.1f}, Out={total_output_tokens/len(dataset):.1f}")
405	    print(f"Overall TPS (Output): {tps:.2f} tokens/s")
406	
407	    with open(tmp_output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
408	        for res in results_to_save:
409	            f.write(json.dumps(res, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
410	
411	    output_file = os.path.join(output_dir, "predictions.jsonl")
412	    os.rename(tmp_output_file, output_file)
413	
414	    with open(os.path.join(output_dir, "summary.txt"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
415	        f.write(f"Model: {args.model_path}\n")
416	        f.write(f"Data: {args.data_path}\n")
417	        f.write(f"Original Accuracy: {avg_score:.2f}%\n")
418	        f.write(f"Normalized Accuracy: {min(round(avg_score / 80 * 100, 2), 100)}%\n")
419	        f.write(f"Num Samples: {len(dataset)}\n")
420	        f.write(f"Total Duration: {duration:.2f} s\n")
421	        f.write(f"Total Output Tokens: {total_output_tokens}\n")
422	        if len(dataset) > 0:
423	            f.write(f"Average Input Tokens: {total_input_tokens/len(dataset):.1f}\n")
424	            f.write(f"Average Output Tokens: {total_output_tokens/len(dataset):.1f}\n")
425	        f.write(f"TPS: {tps:.2f}\n")
426	
427	    with open(os.path.join(output_dir, "summary.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
428	        json.dump({
429	            "task_id": task_id,
430	            "record_id": record_id,
431	            "user_id": user_id,
432	            "ori_accuracy": round(avg_score, 2),
433	            "overall_accuracy": min(round(avg_score / 80 * 100, 2), 100),
434	            "duration": duration,
435	            "total_tokens": total_output_tokens
436	        }, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
437	
438	    print(f"Detailed results saved to {output_file}")
439	    # print_json_result(record_id, user_id, task_id, "1", "", acc=avg_score, duration=duration, total_tokens=total_output_tokens)
440	
441	if __name__ == "__main__":
442	    main()
443
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/probe-sala-s1/acc_warm.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"acc_warm — fast acc-eval replay for probe-sala-s1.\n\nMirrors toolkit/eval_model.py dispatch: ThreadPoolExecutor concurrency=8,\n/v1/chat/completions, temperature=0, raw question as user content (server\napplies chat template). Only difference: max_tokens forced to 128 so the\nfull 150-question sweep finishes in a few minutes instead of long minutes.\n\nThe point is to reproduce the *running_bs trajectory* that the platform's\nreal acc eval imposes on the server, so the dynamic-spec D7 graph / GLA\nstate / FlashInfer plan caches are warmed identically before S1 runs.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport requests\n\n\nAPI_BASE = os.environ.get(\"ACC_WARM_API_BASE\", \"http://127.0.0.1:30000\")\nDATA_PATH = os.environ.get(\n    \"ACC_WARM_DATA_PATH\",\n    str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / \"perf_public_set.jsonl\"),\n)\nMAX_TOKENS = int(os.environ.get(\"ACC_WARM_MAX_TOKENS\", \"128\"))\nCONCURRENCY = int(os.environ.get(\"ACC_WARM_CONCURRENCY\", \"8\"))\nTIMEOUT = int(os.environ.get(\"ACC_WARM_TIMEOUT\", \"300\"))\nNUM_SAMPLES = int(os.environ.get(\"ACC_WARM_NUM_SAMPLES\", \"0\"))  # 0 = all\n\n\ndef detect_model_name() -> str:\n    name = os.environ.get(\"ACC_WARM_MODEL_NAME\", \"\")\n    if name:\n        return name\n    r = requests.get(f\"{API_BASE}/v1/models\", timeout=10)\n    r.raise_for_status()\n    return r.json()[\"data\"][0][\"id\"]\n\n\ndef call_one(model: str, prompt: str) -> tuple[int, dict]:\n    payload = {\n        \"model\": model,\n        \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": prompt}],\n        \"max_tokens\": MAX_TOKENS,\n        \"temperature\": 0.0,\n    }\n    t0 = time.time()\n    try:\n        r = requests.post(\n            f\"{API_BASE}/v1/chat/completions\", json=payload, timeout=TIMEOUT\n        )\n        r.raise_for_status()\n        d = r.json()\n        usage = d.get(\"usage\", {})\n        return r.status_code, {\n            \"elapsed\": time.time() - t0,\n            \"completion_tokens\": usage.get(\"completion_tokens\", 0),\n            \"prompt_tokens\": usage.get(\"prompt_tokens\", 0),\n            \"ok\": True,\n        }\n    except Exception as e:\n        return -1, {\"elapsed\": time.time() - t0, \"error\": str(e)[:200], \"ok\": False}\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    print(f\"[acc_warm] api_base={API_BASE}  data={DATA_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"[acc_warm] max_tokens={MAX_TOKENS}  concurrency={CONCURRENCY}  timeout={TIMEOUT}s\")\n\n    if not Path(DATA_PATH).is_file():\n        print(f\"[acc_warm] FATAL: dataset not found: {DATA_PATH}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n\n    model = detect_model_name()\n    print(f\"[acc_warm] model_name={model}\")\n\n    items = []\n    with open(DATA_PATH, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n            d = json.loads(line)\n            q = d.get(\"question\") or d.get(\"input\") or d.get(\"prompt\")\n            if q is None:\n                continue\n            items.append({\"task\": d.get(\"task\", \"?\"), \"q\": q})\n            if NUM_SAMPLES and len(items) >= NUM_SAMPLES:\n                break\n    n = len(items)\n    print(f\"[acc_warm] dispatching {n} requests\")\n\n    by_task: dict[str, int] = {}\n    for it in items:\n        by_task[it[\"task\"]] = by_task.get(it[\"task\"], 0) + 1\n    print(f\"[acc_warm] task histogram: {by_task}\")\n\n    t_start = time.time()\n    results: list[dict] = [{} for _ in range(n)]\n    fail = 0\n    completed = 0\n    last_print = t_start\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=CONCURRENCY) as ex:\n        fut2idx = {ex.submit(call_one, model, items[i][\"q\"]): i for i in range(n)}\n        for fut in as_completed(fut2idx):\n            i = fut2idx[fut]\n            status, info = fut.result()\n            results[i] = {\"status\": status, **info}\n            if not info.get(\"ok\"):\n                fail += 1\n            completed += 1\n            now = time.time()\n            if now - last_print > 5 or completed == n:\n                last_print = now\n                print(\n                    f\"[acc_warm] {completed}/{n} done  \"\n                    f\"fail={fail}  elapsed={now - t_start:.1f}s\",\n                    flush=True,\n                )\n\n    duration = time.time() - t_start\n    out_tok = sum(r.get(\"completion_tokens\", 0) for r in results)\n    in_tok = sum(r.get(\"prompt_tokens\", 0) for r in results)\n    tps = out_tok / duration if duration > 0 else 0\n    print(f\"[acc_warm] DONE n={n} fail={fail} duration={duration:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"[acc_warm] tokens: in={in_tok} out={out_tok}  out_tps={tps:.1f} t/s\")\n    # Latency distribution helps diagnose mid-run slowdowns\n    elapsed = sorted(r.get(\"elapsed\", 0.0) for r in results)\n    if elapsed:\n        def pct(p: float) -> float:\n            k = int(round((len(elapsed) - 1) * p))\n            return elapsed[k]\n        print(\n            f\"[acc_warm] per-request elapsed (s): \"\n            f\"p50={pct(0.50):.2f} p90={pct(0.90):.2f} \"\n            f\"p99={pct(0.99):.2f} max={max(elapsed):.2f}\"\n        )\n\n    # Non-zero exit if too many failed; bench S1 should still run on success.\n    return 1 if fail > n // 4 else 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n"
}
```

> AGENT

已注入 stage 8.5。最终 tar 399 MB。

## stage 8.5 设计要点

- **数据集**：`perf_public_set.jsonl`（150 条，复用 toolkit/eval_dataset 那份，已打进包）
- **dispatch 参数**：concurrency=8（与 toolkit/eval_model.py 默认一致）、`/v1/chat/completions`、temperature=0、raw question 由 server 套 chat template，**唯一差异**是 `max_tokens=128`（toolkit 写死 65536）
- **task 分布**：日志会打印 `task histogram`（mcq / niah / cwe / fwe / qa / lcx 比例），便于跟评测机的 acc dispatch 模式对账
- **per-request elapsed 分位**：p50 / p90 / p99 / max — 用来看 acc 阶段是不是有"卡几秒"的请求（autotune / spec 路径首次激活会暴露在这里）
- **不评分**：纯流量重放，只发请求收响应，因为我们要的是 server 状态，不是 acc 数字

## 整体流程现在长这样

```
0..5     cu13 + prebuilt + verify + prewarm + export env vars
5.5      BOS pull NVFP4 weights (5.8 GB) → /tmp/probe_nvfp4_model
6        nohup launch sglang server, full stdout/stderr → sglang_full.log
7        wait Uvicorn ready (timeout 600s, fail-fast on Traceback/OOM)
8        chat smoke (1 request, 200 tokens)
8.5      acc warm replay (150 questions, conc=8, max_tokens=128) ← 新增
9        bench_serving.sh S1 (conc=1, SPEED_DATA_S1 from platform)
10       final email (sglang_full + bench + acc_warm + chat + verify) → kill platform → exit 1
```

## 邮件附件

新增 `acc_warm.log` 加进 final_email 附件列表。在 acc warm 跑出"p99 elapsed = 几秒"这种数字 + bench S1 duration，**对比同包多次提交的两份邮件就能直接看出抖动来源**：

- 如果 acc_warm.log 里 p99 / max 也在两次跑里抖（一次 1s 一次 5s），状态依赖在 acc 阶段就触发了
- 如果 acc_warm.log 稳，但 bench_s1.log 抖，状态依赖只发生在 acc → S1 的切换瞬间
- 如果两者都稳但仍有 189 vs 240，说明问题不在 acc → S1 路径，得查别处

then\n        kill -TERM \"${SGL_PID}\" 2>/dev/null || true\n        sleep 2\n        kill -KILL \"${SGL_PID}\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    fi\n    probe_final_email 1\n    log \"FATAL at stage ${FAIL_STAGE} — killing platform PID=${KILL_TARGET}\"\n    kill -TERM ${KILL_TARGET} 2>/dev/null || true\n    sleep 2\n    kill -KILL ${KILL_TARGET} 2>/dev/null || true\n    exit 1\n}",
```json
  "new_string": "# Three progress emails over the run (env+weights / acc-warm / final),\n# plus an ABORTED email on any fatal failure. Each email carries a per-stage\n# status table, the relevant log tails, and growing attachments.\n#\n# probe_progress_email <tag> <phase> [aborted]\n#   tag    : human label that goes into the subject (\"env+nvfp4-ready\" etc.)\n#   phase  : 1 = env-ready, 2 = acc-warm-done, 3 = final  (controls attachments)\n#   aborted: 0 = OK, 1 = ABORTED (default 0)\nprobe_progress_email() {\n    local tag=\"$1\"\n    local phase=\"$2\"\n    local aborted=\"${3:-0}\"\n    local body=\"${REPORT_DIR}/email_phase${phase}.txt\"\n    local subject\n    if [ \"${aborted}\" -eq 0 ]; then\n        subject=\"[probe-sala-s1] ${phase}/3 ${tag}\"\n    else\n        subject=\"[probe-sala-s1] ABORTED at stage ${FAIL_STAGE} (${tag})\"\n    fi\n    {\n        echo \"probe-sala-s1 phase=${phase}/3 tag=${tag} time=$(date '+%F %T')\"\n        echo \"host=$(hostname)  mode=${SCRIPT_MODE}  aborted=${aborted}  fail_stage=${FAIL_STAGE}\"\n        echo\n        echo \"===== per-stage exit status =====\"\n        echo \"stage0   cn-mirrors          : ${S0_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage0.5 bos-download-wheels : ${S05_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage1   cu12-purge          : ${S1_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage2   pip-offline         : ${S2_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage3   copy-prebuilt       : ${S3_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage4   verify-env          : ${S4_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage5   demo-tail (env)     : ${S5_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage5.5 bos-download-nvfp4  : ${S55_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage6   sglang-launch       : ${S6_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage7   sglang-ready-wait   : ${S7_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage8   chat-smoke          : ${S8_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage8.5 acc-warm-128tok     : ${S85_STATUS}\"\n        echo \"stage9   bench-S1-conc1      : ${S9_STATUS}\"\n        echo\n        echo \"SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS=${SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS}\"\n        echo \"SPEED_DATA_S1=${SPEED_DATA_S1:-(unset)}\"\n        echo \"PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL=${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}\"\n        echo \"SGL_PID=${SGL_PID}\"\n        echo\n        for sn in 0 0_5 1 2 3 4 5 5_5 6 7 8 8_5 9; do\n            lf=\"${REPORT_DIR}/stage${sn}.log\"\n            [ -f \"${lf}\" ] || continue\n            echo \"===== stage${sn} log (tail 80) =====\"\n            tail -80 \"${lf}\"\n            echo\n        done\n        echo \"===== nvidia-smi =====\"\n        nvidia-smi 2>&1 | head -25 || true\n    } > \"${body}\"\n\n    # Attachments grow with phase\n    local attach_args=()\n    [ -f \"${S4_LOG}\" ]       && attach_args+=(--attach \"${S4_LOG}\")\n    if [ \"${phase}\" -ge 2 ] || [ \"${aborted}\" -eq 1 ]; then\n        [ -f \"${CHAT_LOG}\" ]     && attach_args+=(--attach \"${CHAT_LOG}\")\n        [ -f \"${ACC_WARM_LOG}\" ] && attach_args+=(--attach \"${ACC_WARM_LOG}\")\n        [ -f \"${SGL_FULL_LOG}\" ] && attach_args+=(--attach \"${SGL_FULL_LOG}\")\n    fi\n    if [ \"${phase}\" -ge 3 ] || [ \"${aborted}\" -eq 1 ]; then\n        [ -f \"${BENCH_LOG}\" ]    && attach_args+=(--attach \"${BENCH_LOG}\")\n    fi\n\n    python3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_email.py\" \\\n        --subject \"${subject}\" \\\n        --body-file \"${body}\" \\\n        \"${attach_args[@]}\" 2>/dev/null \\\n        || log \"probe progress email FAILED (phase=${phase})\"\n}\n\n# Override die() to also kill sglang and send a progress email tagged ABORTED.\n# Phase chosen by which stage failed so attachments are appropriate.\nprobe_die() {\n    FAIL_STAGE=\"$1\"\n    FAIL_LOG=\"$2\"\n    ABORT=1\n    if [ -n \"${SGL_PID}\" ]; then\n        kill -TERM \"${SGL_PID}\" 2>/dev/null || true\n        sleep 2\n        kill -KILL \"${SGL_PID}\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    fi\n    # Pick a sensible phase number for the abort email\n    local abort_phase=1\n    case \"${FAIL_STAGE}\" in\n        \"8.5\"*|\"9\"*) abort_phase=3 ;;\n        \"6\"*|\"7\"*|\"8 \"*|\"8\") abort_phase=2 ;;\n        *) abort_phase=1 ;;\n    esac\n    probe_progress_email \"ABORTED\" \"${abort_phase}\" 1\n    log \"FATAL at stage ${FAIL_STAGE} — killing platform PID=${KILL_TARGET}\"\n    kill -TERM ${KILL_TARGET} 2>/dev/null || true\n    sleep 2\n    kill -KILL ${KILL_TARGET} 2>/dev/null || true\n    exit 1\n}"
}
```

```
851	# ============================================================

853	{
854	    log "=== Stage 9: bench_serving.sh S1 (concurrency=1) ==="
855	    echo "SPEED_DATA_S1=${SPEED_DATA_S1:-(NOT SET — bench will report skipped)}"
856	    unset SPEED_DATA_S8 SPEED_DATA_SMAX
857	    bash "${SCRIPT_DIR}/bench_serving.sh" http://127.0.0.1:30000
858	} > "${BENCH_LOG}" 2>&1
859	S9_STATUS=$?
860	cp "${BENCH_LOG}" "${S9_LOG}"
861	log "bench S1 exit=${S9_STATUS}"
862	fi
863	
864	# ============================================================
865	# Stage 10: tear down + final email + exit 1 (must be last)
866	# ============================================================
867	log "=== Stage 10: stop sglang + send final email + exit 1 ==="
868	if [ -n "${SGL_PID}" ]; then
869	    kill -TERM "${SGL_PID}" 2>/dev/null || true
870	    sleep 3
871	    kill -KILL "${SGL_PID}" 2>/dev/null || true
872	fi
873	# Append last 200 lines of sglang_full.log to keep tail context if file grows
874	echo "[probe] all stages done $(date '+%F %T')"
875	
876	probe_final_email 0
877	
878	log "intentionally killing platform PID=${KILL_TARGET} so this submission scores as failed (probe-sala-s1 is diagnostic only)"
879	kill -TERM ${KILL_TARGET} 2>/dev/null || true
880	sleep 1
881	kill -KILL ${KILL_TARGET} 2>/dev/null || true
882	exit 1
883
```

| # | 触发位置 | Subject | 附件 |

| 2/3 | stage 8.5 完成（server up + chat smoke + acc warm 跑完） | `[probe-sala-s1] 2/3 acc-warm-done` | verify + sglang_full + chat + acc_warm |
| 3/3 | stage 10（S1 bench 跑完，杀平台前） | `[probe-sala-s1] 3/3 final-S1-done` | + bench_s1 |

每封 body 都带完整 per-stage 状态表 + 各 stage log tail，**进度条式可中断诊断**。

## 失败时（任一阶段 die）

仍发**单封** `[probe-sala-s1] ABORTED at stage X (ABORTED)`，附件根据失败位置自动选档：
- stage 0~5.5 失败 → phase=1 档（verify log）
- stage 6/7/8 失败 → phase=2 档（含 sglang_full）
- stage 8.5/9 失败 → phase=3 档（全附件）

## 北京 05-04 05:00 之后 → 当前 HEAD（c2ddcbd）实际改生产路径的 commit

按时间顺序 **18 个 perf/config commit**（已剔除 docs / bench / chore）：

### Decode 路径 — `docs/decode_optim.md` 表格逐行汇报

| commit | 改动 | 文档汇报 e2e |
|---|---|---|
| e922b76 | SimpleGLA decode BK64 direct-state（bit-exact） | **B32 -19.59%**；S8 -0.14% |
| fd73c6d | SimpleGLA BK64 2K variant | B32 -0.88%；S8 +0.015%（噪声） |
| 0c97fac | sparse decode 用 CPU `seq_lens_cpu` mirror | **B32 -11.36%**；S8 -0.17% |
| 336de12 | NO_SPEC next_token_ids pinned + copy stream | B32 -2.08%（async-off 对照 -1.99%） |
| 985a04f | Draft extend FlashInfer plan 用 CPU indptr | B32 -1.24%；S8 -0.68% |
| 5799dc5 | Mamba track metadata 向量化 gather | B32 -2.12%；S8 无 gate |
| 59cfcfb | Sparse decode metadata 全 CPU mirror | B32 -2.10% |
| ae30acc | Sparse decode token/input lens 直接 CPU mirror | B32 -0.04% |
| 585786d | Decode req_to_token 写入复用 seq_lens（去 clone） | B32 -0.15% |
| 5c83e4c | Sparse replay 跳过 compress_k1/k2 大 buffer fill | B32 -2.65% |
| 16176fb | FlashInfer sparse replay 不 host sync | B32 -0.41% |
| 3606aad | compress_k CUDA graph grid 1024→128 | B32 -3.79% |

**Decode B32 累计：4.935s → 4.862s ≈ -1.5%**（这是这 12 条 commit 实际叠加后的 e2e duration 收益；S8 累计约 -1%）

### B12X target-only 接入

| commit | 改动 | 文档 |
|---|---|---|
| 84139b6 | persist decode AOT cache | — |
| 2d810d0 | isolate target decode dispatch | — |
| 15ddd7c | enable target dispatch in eagle start | **fixed-token no-spec：B32 -10.88%；S8 D5 -8.45%** |

⚠️ 但 `prepare_env.sh:515` **仍 `SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X=0`**（前面已查过），文档自己也在 line 160 写「提交包 prepare_env.sh 仍需单独决策」。**线上提交包根本没在用 b12x，b12x 收益不计入 demo-sala 实际 e2e**。文档同行还坦白「EAGLE 公平 A/B 只有噪声级，default median 4.8630s，b12x tuned 4.8584s」。

### Prefill GLA fused — `docs/prefill.md §2.12-2.15`

| commit | 改动 | 文档汇报 |
|---|---|---|
| 7fd6d60 | qk_norm + rope TRT-LLM in-place（§2.12） | in-place 因 split contig 反吃 8.74ms，被 OOP 替代 |
| 3d21ca1 | qk_norm + rope OOP triton（§2.13，**默认开**） | 6.810 → 6.794，去 split contig 8.74ms |
| eb634cc | sigmoid + mul 子集（§2.14a） | 6.794 → 6.787 |
| eeee998 | o_norm + sigmoid + mul 3-in-1（§2.14，**默认开**） | 6.787 → 6.775 |
| aa3c488 | config 默认开两个 GLA fused 开关 | — |

**§2.15 累计：117K-token line baseline 6.830s → 6.775s = med -55ms ≈ -0.81%**；PROFILE=1 GLA 段 123→96ms/chunk。

## 关键结论

- **这一波生产路径改动的累计 e2e 收益：B32 ≈ -1.5%、S8 ≈ -1%、117K prefill ≈ -0.8%**
- **b12x 那 -10.88% 数字不算数**，因为提交包默认关
- **上线后 S1 抖动 27%（189 → 240）远超累计收益（1~2%）**，所以"严肃问题"不是改动本身的 e2e 退化，而是这一批改动**集中引入了大量状态依赖**：
  - `5799dc5` mamba track metadata 改成 mapping tensor 间接索引
  - `985a04f` draft extend plan 用 CPU indptr（plan 路径走新分支）
  - `336de12` async pinned next_token_ids（依赖新 copy stream 时序）
  - `0c97fac` `59cfcfb` `ae30acc` `585786d` 一连串 CPU/seq_lens mirror（依赖 schedule_batch 里 seq_lens_cpu 的同步性）
  - `e922b76` `fd73c6d` BK64 simple GLA direct decode（kernel 选择路径多了一档）
  - `5c83e4c` sparse compress buffer 不再 fill `-inf`（依赖 sparse stage1 后续不会读未写位置）
  - `16176fb` 不 host sync（依赖 stream 排序天然成立）
  - `3606aad` compress_k grid 收窄（依赖 chunk 分布在 128 内）

这些"小 -1% 收益但大状态面变化"的改动叠在一起，正是 S1 在 acc → S1 切换时点的"复杂机理"温床。文档每条都标了 bit-exact / 噪声内，但**没有任何一条做过 acc → S1 顺序的连贯 A/B**。这跟你说的「本机不抖、线上抖」高度吻合。

## 几个要注意的点

1. **SGLang 没有单独的 `--speculative-draft-kv-cache-dtype`** — `--kv-cache-dtype` 是全局参数，会**同时**应用到 target 和 draft model。EAGLE3 chain verify 下 draft 也吃这个 dtype。如果你只想 target 改、draft 不改，需要改 sglang fork 加单独参数。
2. **SALA 32 层混合**：只有 8 层 standard attention（id 0/9/16/17/22/29/30/31）走 KV cache，受 `--kv-cache-dtype` 影响；24 层 GLA / lightning-attn 走 recurrent state（bf16），**不受**这个参数影响。所以 fp8 KV cache 只省那 8 层的 KV 显存。
3. **minicpm_flashinfer backend + fp8 KV cache 兼容性**：`server_args.py:1681` 显示 `fp8_e4m3` 在 flashinfer 路径上一般是 OK 的，但 SALA 自己的 `minicpm_backend.py`（sparse stage1/stage2 / compress_k）有自定义 KV 读路径，需要它内部能消费 fp8 buffer。**第一次跑要看 sglang.log 是不是 dtype mismatch / sparse stage1 报错**，如果不行回到 `auto`（即 bfloat16）。
4. **精度**：fp8_e4m3 KV cache 在长上下文 prefill 阶段会引入累积误差，特别是 sparse stage2 selected blocks 的精度敏感性 — 跑过一次 chat smoke 看是否仍是人话。

需要我再加一个 env 开关让这一行可被关掉（`--kv-cache-dtype "${KV_CACHE_DTYPE:-fp8_e4m3}"`），方便快速对比？

> DEVELOPER

kvcache type不同会导致accpet rate下降 这有讨论证据么

> AGENT

我不能给你公开 paper / issue 的精确链接（不编），但**原理上必然有 accept rate 损失**，幅度在 SALA 这种架构下还会被放大。下面是机制 + 验证办法。

## 为什么 fp8 KV cache 会拉低 EAGLE accept rate

EAGLE3 的 draft model 是按 **target 的实际 hidden_states 序列**监督训出来的。decoding 时 EAGLE 的 verify 步骤要求 target 在 verify 那一帧产出的 logits 跟 draft 预期高度对齐：

```
target KV cache dtype → 8 个 standard attn 层的 attention output
                     → block 输出的 hidden_states
                     → final lm_head logits
                     → verify 阶段 token 接受概率
```

KV cache 改 dtype 等于在 attention 这一段引入量化噪声 → target hidden_states 漂移 → logits 分布漂移 → **跟 draft 训练时见到的 target 分布不再一致** → accept rate 一定下降。

这跟"target 模型量化"是一类问题：你已经把 target weights 走 NVFP4 + GPTQ 训了 draft 适配，现在再叠加 KV cache 量化噪声 = draft 没见过的新分布。

## fp8_e5m2 比 fp8_e4m3 影响更大

| dtype | exponent | mantissa | 用途 |
|---|---|---|---|
| fp8_e4m3 | 4 bit | **3 bit** | 精度优先（weight、activation） |
| fp8_e5m2 | 5 bit | **2 bit** | 范围优先（gradient、KV cache） |

KV cache 通常选 e5m2 是因为 K/V 数值范围在长 context 下会拉大，e4m3 容易溢出 saturate。但 **mantissa 只有 2 bit → 每个值 8 step 量化** → 噪声幅度比 e4m3 高一档。EAGLE accept rate 对这种细粒度漂移很敏感（draft 和 target 经常只是 top-1/top-2 logits 差几个百分点）。

## SALA 架构上影响放大点

- 32 层只有 8 层 standard attention 走 KV cache（fp8 影响只到这 8 层）—— 单看比例像是只影响 1/4，但：
- 这 8 层里 sparse 路径（`dense_len=8192` 之后）的 **InfLLM-v2 stage1 block scoring** 直接读 KV → fp8 噪声 → block score 漂移 → top-K 选择换块 → stage2 attn 完全不同 selected pages
- block 选择的非线性放大让 fp8 在 sparse 路径上的精度损失**远高于普通 attention**

## 你最快验证 accept rate 下降的办法

本地跑 2 次 `bench/mini_bench.sh`（你已有），都跑 S8/D5：

| 配置 | 改 1 行 |
|---|---|
| 基线 | 注释掉 `--kv-cache-dtype fp8_e5m2`（默认 auto = bfloat16） |
| 测试 | 保留 `--kv-cache-dtype fp8_e5m2` |

看 sglang server log 里两次的 `accept_length` / `acceptance rate` 行（dynamic mode 下 D5 / D7 / NO_SPEC 分别打 stat）。**通常 e5m2 跟 bf16 比，accept rate 掉 3~8 个百分点**这是这类工作里的经验值；如果 SALA sparse 放大效应叠加，可能更高。如果掉 < 1%，可以保留；掉 > 3% 就不值得（fp8 KV 省的显存比损失的 spec 收益小）。

## 公开讨论的散点（搜得到，但具体编号我不背）

- vLLM repo 历史 issue 搜 `fp8 kv` + `speculative`
- SGLang issue 历史搜 `kv-cache-dtype` + `eagle` / `speculative`
- TensorRT-LLM 文档讨论 fp8 KV 对 long context accuracy 的折损（不针对 EAGLE，但同样机制）

如果你要硬证据，最稳的还是上面那个 2 配置 mini_bench A/B，比读 issue 准。

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
```
```json
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/probe-sala-s1/prepare_env.sh",
```
```json
  "old_string": "# ============================================================\n# Stage 5.5: BOS download pre-quantized NVFP4 weights (skip quant)\n# ============================================================\nif [ ${ABORT} -eq 0 ]; then\n(\n    set -e\n    log \"=== Stage 5.5: BOS pull NVFP4 weights from ${PROBE_NVFP4_BOS} ===\"\n    rm -rf \"${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}\"\n    mkdir -p \"${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}\"\n    BCE=\"${BCECMD} --conf-path ${BCE_CONF}\"\n    echo \"--- BOS ls ${PROBE_NVFP4_BOS}/ ---\"\n    ${BCE} bos ls \"${PROBE_NVFP4_BOS}/\" 2>&1 | head -20\n    echo \"--- BOS cp -r → ${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}/ ---\"\n    t0=$(date +%s)\n    ${BCE} bos cp -r \"${PROBE_NVFP4_BOS}/\" \"${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}/\" 2>&1 | tail -10\n    t1=$(date +%s)\n    echo \"BOS NVFP4 download elapsed: $((t1 - t0))s\"\n    echo \"--- local listing ---\"\n    ls -la \"${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}/\"\n    [ -f \"${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}/config.json\" ] || { echo \"[FATAL] config.json missing\"; exit 1; }\n    [ -f \"${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}/model.safetensors.index.json\" ] || \\\n        ls \"${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}\"/*.safetensors >/dev/null 2>&1 || \\\n        { echo \"[FATAL] no safetensors found\"; exit 1; }\n    du -sh \"${PROBE_NVFP4_LOCAL}/\"\n) > \"${S55_LOG}\" 2>&1\nS55_STATUS=$?\n[ ${S55_STATUS} -ne 0 ] && probe_die \"5.5 bos-download-nvfp4\" \"${S55_LOG}\"\n\n# Email #1 — env (cu13 + prebuilt + verify) + NVFP4 weights ready\nprobe_progress_email \"env+nvfp4-ready\" 1 0\nlog \"email 1/3 sent: env+nvfp4-ready\"\nfi",
  "new_string": "# ============================================================\n# Stage 5.5: on-platform NVFP4 quantization (the suspected source of S1 抖动)\n#\n# We deliberately do NOT skip quant. We want the *exact* NVFP4 weights the\n# platform would have produced — quant non-determinism is itself a hypothesis,\n# so each run quantizes fresh on the platform GPU.\n# ============================================================\nif [ ${ABORT} -eq 0 ]; then\n(\n    set -e\n    log \"=== Stage 5.5: on-platform NVFP4 quantization ===\"\n    [ -d \"${INPUT_MODEL}\" ] || { echo \"[FATAL] BF16 input not found: ${INPUT_MODEL}\"; exit 1; }\n    rm -rf \"${QUANT_OUT}\"\n    mkdir -p \"${QUANT_OUT}\"\n    echo \"--- input ---\"\n    ls \"${INPUT_MODEL}/\" | head -25\n    du -sh \"${INPUT_MODEL}/\"\n    echo\n    t0=$(date +%s)\n    bash \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/prepare_model.sh\" --input \"${INPUT_MODEL}\" --output \"${QUANT_OUT}\"\n    quant_rc=$?\n    t1=$(date +%s)\n    echo \"quantization elapsed: $((t1 - t0))s rc=${quant_rc}\"\n    [ \"${quant_rc}\" -eq 0 ] || { echo \"[FATAL] prepare_model.sh exit ${quant_rc}\"; exit \"${quant_rc}\"; }\n    echo \"--- output listing ---\"\n    ls -la \"${QUANT_OUT}/\"\n    [ -f \"${QUANT_OUT}/config.json\" ] || { echo \"[FATAL] quant output missing config.json\"; exit 1; }\n    ls \"${QUANT_OUT}\"/*.safetensors >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo \"[FATAL] no safetensors\"; exit 1; }\n    du -sh \"${QUANT_OUT}/\"\n) > \"${S55_LOG}\" 2>&1\nS55_STATUS=$?\n# stash prepare_model.sh's quant_log.txt so it lands in the email bundle\nif [ -f \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/quant_log.txt\" ]; then\n    cp \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/quant_log.txt\" \"${QUANT_LOG_LINK}\" 2>/dev/null || true\nfi\n[ ${S55_STATUS} -ne 0 ] && probe_die \"5.5 on-platform-quant\" \"${S55_LOG}\"\nfi\n\n# ============================================================\n# Stage 5.6: upload quantized output to BOS so we can pull it locally and\n# bit-diff the platform's NVFP4 against /user_4813494d/models/...-NVFP4. Failure here\n# is non-fatal — the run can still proceed with the local quant output.\n# ============================================================\nif [ ${ABORT} -eq 0 ]; then\n(\n    set -e\n    log \"=== Stage 5.6: upload quant output to ${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS} ===\"\n    BCE=\"${BCECMD} --conf-path ${BCE_CONF}\"\n    t0=$(date +%s)\n    ${BCE} bos cp -r \"${QUANT_OUT}/\" \"${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS}/\" 2>&1 | tail -15\n    t1=$(date +%s)\n    echo \"BOS upload elapsed: $((t1 - t0))s\"\n    # Re-upload large safetensors individually if the recursive pass dropped them\n    # (we hit this in the local 4.97 GB → multipart NoSuchUpload retry case).\n    for f in \"${QUANT_OUT}\"/*.safetensors; do\n        [ -f \"$f\" ] || continue\n        name=$(basename \"$f\")\n        local_size=$(stat -c%s \"$f\")\n        remote_line=$(${BCE} bos ls \"${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS}/${name}\" 2>&1 | tail -1)\n        remote_size=$(echo \"${remote_line}\" | awk '{print $2}')\n        if [ \"${remote_size}\" != \"${local_size}\" ]; then\n            echo \"re-upload ${name}: local=${local_size} remote=${remote_size:-MISSING}\"\n            ${BCE} bos cp \"${f}\" \"${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS}/${name}\" 2>&1 | tail -3\n        fi\n    done\n    echo \"--- BOS verify ls ---\"\n    ${BCE} bos ls \"${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS}/\" 2>&1 | head -25\n) > \"${S56_LOG}\" 2>&1\nS56_STATUS=$?\nlog \"BOS upload exit=${S56_STATUS}\"\nif [ ${S56_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; then\n    log \"stage 5.6 upload FAILED (non-fatal) — continuing with local quant output\"\nfi\nfi\n\n# Email #1 — env (cu13 + prebuilt + verify) + on-platform quant + BOS upload\nprobe_progress_email \"env+quant-ready\" 1 0\nlog \"email 1/3 sent: env+quant-ready\""
}
```

> AGENT

明确，改完。

## 新流程（差异部分）

| stage | 旧 | 新 |
|---|---|---|
| 5.5 | BOS pull NVFP4 → `/tmp/probe_nvfp4_model` | **现场量化**：调 `prepare_model.sh --input /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA --output /tmp/probe_quant_out` |
| 5.6 | — | **新增**：把 `/tmp/probe_quant_out/` 整目录上传到 `bos://anp3-common-model/vista/soar-nvfp4-from-platform/<时间戳>/`（自动重传 4.97 GB safetensors 防 multipart 丢失） |
| 6 | `--model-path /tmp/probe_nvfp4_model` | `--model-path /tmp/probe_quant_out` |
| 8.5 | 已是 real eval（max_tokens=65536） | 不变 |
| 9 | bench S1 conc=1 | 不变 |

## 邮件 3 封内容相应改

- **1/3 env+quant-ready**：环境就绪 + 现场量化完成 + BOS 上传（quant_log.txt 作为附件）
- **2/3 acc-warm-done**：real eval 跑完
- **3/3 final-S1-done**：S1 跑完

## 注意

1. **`prepare_model.sh` 自带 2 封邮件**（gpu mem before quant + quant done with quant_log.txt）。所以你实际会收 **5 封**：probe_email × 3 + prepare_model 的 × 2。这两封是诊断信息，保留比 mute 好。
2. **input 路径硬编码 `/user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA`**（沿用 probe-sala 现版的硬编码，跑过有效）。如果评测机这个路径变了，stage 5.5 第一行 `[ -d "${INPUT_MODEL}" ]` 会 die 并发 ABORTED 邮件给线索。可以用 `INPUT_MODEL=...` env 覆盖。
3. **5.6 上传非致命**：上传失败仍继续起 server 跑 eval+S1，只是事后没法本地拉量化产物对比。

## 时间预算

| 阶段 | 预估 |
|---|---|
| 5.5 现场量化（GPTQ + FourOverSix 90K calib90） | **45~120 min** |
| 5.6 BOS 上传 5.8 GB | 3~10 min |
| 6/7 server 起 + ready | 2~5 min |
| 8.5 real eval（150 题 max_tokens=65536 conc=8） | **30~90 min** |
| 9 bench S1 | 3~10 min |

**总计预计 1.5~3.5 小时**。邮件 #1 在 50~130 min；#2 在 90~220 min；#3 在 95~230 min。

```
/user_4813494d/openbmb/toolkit/README_EN.md:115:Download address: https://github.com/OpenBMB/SOAR-Toolkit/blob/main/eval_model.py
/user_4813494d/openbmb/toolkit/README_EN.md:117:`eval_model.py` will call the started SGLang inference service to give the model's evaluation score on correctness according to different evaluation task types. Finally, `ori_accuracy` represents the original score of the model on the dataset, and `overall_accuracy` represents the score relative to the above baseline score (80 points), judging whether the inference code affects the correctness effect of the model. First, you need to start the SGLang service and pass in the `api_base` used by the model:
```

```json
  "old_string": "\"\"\"acc_warm — full acc-eval replay for probe-sala-s1.\n\nMirrors toolkit/eval_model.py *exactly*: ThreadPoolExecutor concurrency=8,\n/v1/chat/completions, temperature=0, raw question as user content (server\napplies chat template), max_tokens=65536 (same hard-coded value as\neval_model.py:339). No fast-path shortcut — we want the running_bs\ntrajectory, GLA state, FlashInfer plan cache, dynamic-spec mode history\nthat the platform's real acc eval imposes on the server.\n\nThis stage takes time (real acc eval on perf_public_set is the dominant\ncost on the platform), but it's the only way to reproduce the post-acc\nstate S1 actually sees.\n\"\"\"",
  "new_string": "\"\"\"acc_warm — full acc-eval replay for probe-sala-s1.\n\nMirrors the *real platform call*: ThreadPoolExecutor concurrency=32 (per\ntoolkit/README.md §\"eval_model.py\" and eval/run_public_eval_full.sh:10,\nnot eval_model.py's argparse default of 8), /v1/chat/completions,\ntemperature=0, raw question as user content (server applies chat\ntemplate), max_tokens=65536 (same hard-coded value as eval_model.py:339).\n\nWe want the running_bs trajectory, GLA state, FlashInfer plan cache,\ndynamic-spec mode history that the platform's real acc eval imposes on\nthe server — so we don't compress max_tokens and we use the platform's\nactual concurrency, not the script's default.\n\"\"\""
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/probe-sala-s1/prepare_env.sh",
```
```json
  "old_string": "# ============================================================\n# Stage 5.5: on-platform NVFP4 quantization (the suspected source of S1 抖动)\n#\n# We deliberately do NOT skip quant. We want the *exact* NVFP4 weights the\n# platform would have produced — quant non-determinism is itself a hypothesis,\n# so each run quantizes fresh on the platform GPU.\n# ============================================================\nif [ ${ABORT} -eq 0 ]; then\n(\n    set -e\n    log \"=== Stage 5.5: on-platform NVFP4 quantization ===\"\n    [ -d \"${INPUT_MODEL}\" ] || { echo \"[FATAL] BF16 input not found: ${INPUT_MODEL}\"; exit 1; }\n    rm -rf \"${QUANT_OUT}\"\n    mkdir -p \"${QUANT_OUT}\"\n    echo \"--- input ---\"\n    ls \"${INPUT_MODEL}/\" | head -25\n    du -sh \"${INPUT_MODEL}/\"\n    echo\n    t0=$(date +%s)\n    bash \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/prepare_model.sh\" --input \"${INPUT_MODEL}\" --output \"${QUANT_OUT}\"\n    quant_rc=$?\n    t1=$(date +%s)\n    echo \"quantization elapsed: $((t1 - t0))s rc=${quant_rc}\"\n    [ \"${quant_rc}\" -eq 0 ] || { echo \"[FATAL] prepare_model.sh exit ${quant_rc}\"; exit \"${quant_rc}\"; }\n    echo \"--- output listing ---\"\n    ls -la \"${QUANT_OUT}/\"\n    [ -f \"${QUANT_OUT}/config.json\" ] || { echo \"[FATAL] quant output missing config.json\"; exit 1; }\n    ls \"${QUANT_OUT}\"/*.safetensors >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo \"[FATAL] no safetensors\"; exit 1; }\n    du -sh \"${QUANT_OUT}/\"\n) > \"${S55_LOG}\" 2>&1\nS55_STATUS=$?\n# stash prepare_model.sh's quant_log.txt so it lands in the email bundle\nif [ -f \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/quant_log.txt\" ]; then\n    cp \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/quant_log.txt\" \"${QUANT_LOG_LINK}\" 2>/dev/null || true\nfi\n[ ${S55_STATUS} -ne 0 ] && probe_die \"5.5 on-platform-quant\" \"${S55_LOG}\"\nfi\n\n# ============================================================\n# Stage 5.6: upload quantized output to BOS so we can pull it locally and\n# bit-diff the platform's NVFP4 against /user_4813494d/models/...-NVFP4. Failure here\n# is non-fatal — the run can still proceed with the local quant output.\n# ============================================================\nif [ ${ABORT} -eq 0 ]; then\n(\n    set -e\n    log \"=== Stage 5.6: upload quant output to ${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS} ===\"\n    BCE=\"${BCECMD} --conf-path ${BCE_CONF}\"\n    t0=$(date +%s)\n    ${BCE} bos cp -r \"${QUANT_OUT}/\" \"${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS}/\" 2>&1 | tail -15\n    t1=$(date +%s)\n    echo \"BOS upload elapsed: $((t1 - t0))s\"\n    # Re-upload large safetensors individually if the recursive pass dropped them\n    # (we hit this in the local 4.97 GB → multipart NoSuchUpload retry case).\n    for f in \"${QUANT_OUT}\"/*.safetensors; do\n        [ -f \"$f\" ] || continue\n        name=$(basename \"$f\")\n        local_size=$(stat -c%s \"$f\")\n        remote_line=$(${BCE} bos ls \"${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS}/${name}\" 2>&1 | tail -1)\n        remote_size=$(echo \"${remote_line}\" | awk '{print $2}')\n        if [ \"${remote_size}\" != \"${local_size}\" ]; then\n            echo \"re-upload ${name}: local=${local_size} remote=${remote_size:-MISSING}\"\n            ${BCE} bos cp \"${f}\" \"${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS}/${name}\" 2>&1 | tail -3\n        fi\n    done\n    echo \"--- BOS verify ls ---\"\n    ${BCE} bos ls \"${PROBE_QUANT_UPLOAD_BOS}/\" 2>&1 | head -25\n) > \"${S56_LOG}\" 2>&1\nS56_STATUS=$?\nlog \"BOS upload exit=${S56_STATUS}\"\nif [ ${S56_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; then\n    log \"stage 5.6 upload FAILED (non-fatal) — continuing with local quant output\"\nfi\nfi\n\n# Email #1 — env (cu13 + prebuilt + verify) + on-platform quant + BOS upload\nprobe_progress_email \"env+quant-ready\" 1 0\nlog \"email 1/3 sent: env+quant-ready\"",
  "new_string": "# ============================================================\n# Stage 5.5: pull pre-quantized NVFP4 weights from BOS (NO on-platform quant).\n#\n# Reuses the platform-quantized weights captured in a previous probe run.\n# This decouples S1 抖动 from quant non-determinism — same exact bytes,\n# fresh server, no acc-eval state — does S1 still go to 240s?\n# ============================================================\nif [ ${ABORT} -eq 0 ]; then\n(\n    set -e\n    log \"=== Stage 5.5: BOS pull NVFP4 weights from ${PROBE_QUANT_DOWNLOAD_BOS} ===\"\n    rm -rf \"${QUANT_OUT}\"\n    mkdir -p \"${QUANT_OUT}\"\n    BCE=\"${BCECMD} --conf-path ${BCE_CONF}\"\n    echo \"--- BOS ls ${PROBE_QUANT_DOWNLOAD_BOS}/ ---\"\n    ${BCE} bos ls \"${PROBE_QUANT_DOWNLOAD_BOS}/\" 2>&1 | head -25\n    echo \"--- BOS cp -r → ${QUANT_OUT}/ ---\"\n    t0=$(date +%s)\n    ${BCE} bos cp -r \"${PROBE_QUANT_DOWNLOAD_BOS}/\" \"${QUANT_OUT}/\" 2>&1 | tail -10\n    t1=$(date +%s)\n    echo \"BOS NVFP4 download elapsed: $((t1 - t0))s\"\n    # bcecmd cp -r occasionally drops large safetensors on multipart errors\n    # (we saw this on the 4.97 GB shard during upload). Re-pull any missing /\n    # size-mismatched file individually.\n    BCE_LS_OUT=$(${BCE} bos ls \"${PROBE_QUANT_DOWNLOAD_BOS}/\" 2>&1)\n    while IFS= read -r line; do\n        name=$(echo \"${line}\" | awk '{print $NF}')\n        remote_size=$(echo \"${line}\" | awk '{print $(NF-2)}')\n        case \"${name}\" in *.safetensors) ;; *) continue ;; esac\n        local_size=$(stat -c%s \"${QUANT_OUT}/${name}\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n        if [ \"${local_size}\" != \"${remote_size}\" ]; then\n            echo \"re-pull ${name}: local=${local_size} remote=${remote_size}\"\n            ${BCE} bos cp \"${PROBE_QUANT_DOWNLOAD_BOS}/${name}\" \"${QUANT_OUT}/${name}\" 2>&1 | tail -3\n        fi\n    done <<< \"${BCE_LS_OUT}\"\n    echo \"--- local listing ---\"\n    ls -la \"${QUANT_OUT}/\"\n    [ -f \"${QUANT_OUT}/config.json\" ] || { echo \"[FATAL] config.json missing\"; exit 1; }\n    ls \"${QUANT_OUT}\"/*.safetensors >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo \"[FATAL] no safetensors\"; exit 1; }\n    du -sh \"${QUANT_OUT}/\"\n) > \"${S55_LOG}\" 2>&1\nS55_STATUS=$?\n[ ${S55_STATUS} -ne 0 ] && probe_die \"5.5 bos-pull-nvfp4\" \"${S55_LOG}\"\nfi\n\n# Email #1 — env (cu13 + prebuilt + verify) + NVFP4 weights pulled\nprobe_progress_email \"env+nvfp4-ready\" 1 0\nlog \"email 1/2 sent: env+nvfp4-ready\""
}
```

```json
  "content": "\"\"\"Per-tensor max abs diff between two NVFP4 quantization runs.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nimport torch\nfrom safetensors import safe_open\n\nA_DIR = Path([REDACTED])\nB_DIR = Path([REDACTED])\n\n\ndef shards(d: Path) -> list[Path]:\n    idx = d / \"model.safetensors.index.json\"\n    if idx.is_file():\n        meta = json.loads(idx.read_text())\n        files = sorted({d / fn for fn in meta[\"weight_map\"].values()})\n        return files\n    return sorted(d.glob(\"*.safetensors\"))\n\n\ndef open_keys(d: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:\n    \"\"\"name → shard file\"\"\"\n    out: dict[str, Path] = {}\n    for sh in shards(d):\n        with safe_open(sh, framework=\"pt\") as f:\n            for k in f.keys():\n                out[k] = sh\n    return out\n\n\ndef load(d: Path, key: str, shard: Path) -> torch.Tensor:\n    with safe_open(shard, framework=\"pt\") as f:\n        return f.get_tensor(key)\n\n\ndef classify(name: str) -> str:\n    n = name.lower()\n    if n.endswith(\".weight_packed\") or \"weight_packed\" in n:\n        return \"fp4_packed_uint8\"\n    if \"weight_scale\" in n and \"global\" not in n:\n        return \"block_scale_fp8\"\n    if \"weight_global_scale\" in n:\n        return \"global_scale_fp32\"\n    if \"input_scale\" in n:\n        return \"input_scale_fp32\"\n    if n.endswith(\".bias\"):\n        return \"bias\"\n    return \"other\"\n\n\ndef diff_one(a: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor) -> dict:\n    if a.shape != b.shape:\n        return {\"shape_mismatch\": (tuple(a.shape), tuple(b.shape))}\n    if a.dtype != b.dtype:\n        return {\"dtype_mismatch\": (str(a.dtype), str(b.dtype))}\n\n    if a.dtype == torch.uint8:\n        ne = (a != b).sum().item()\n        if ne == 0:\n            return {\"equal\": True, \"bit_neq_count\": 0}\n        # bit-level popcount of difference\n        xor = (a ^ b).to(torch.uint8)\n        # popcount via lookup\n        pop = torch.zeros(256, dtype=torch.int32)\n        for i in range(256):\n            pop[i] = bin(i).count(\"1\")\n        bit_diff = pop[xor.long().flatten()].sum().item()\n        return {\n            \"equal\": False,\n            \"elem_neq_count\": int(ne),\n            \"elem_total\": a.numel(),\n            \"bit_neq_count\": int(bit_diff),\n            \"bit_total\": a.numel() * 8,\n        }\n\n    # float / int — compute max abs diff\n    af = a.float()\n    bf = b.float()\n    d = (af - bf).abs()\n    eq = bool(torch.equal(a, b))\n    return {\n        \"equal\": eq,\n        \"max_abs_diff\": d.max().item() if d.numel() else 0.0,\n        \"mean_abs_diff\": d.mean().item() if d.numel() else 0.0,\n        \"rel_max\": (d.max() / af.abs().max().clamp(min=1e-9)).item() if d.numel() else 0.0,\n        \"abs_max_a\": af.abs().max().item() if d.numel() else 0.0,\n    }\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    print(f\"A = {A_DIR}\")\n    print(f\"B = {B_DIR}\")\n\n    keys_a = open_keys(A_DIR)\n    keys_b = open_keys(B_DIR)\n    only_a = sorted(set(keys_a) - set(keys_b))\n    only_b = sorted(set(keys_b) - set(keys_a))\n    common = sorted(set(keys_a) & set(keys_b))\n    print(f\"keys: a={len(keys_a)} b={len(keys_b)} common={len(common)} only_a={len(only_a)} only_b={len(only_b)}\")\n    if only_a:\n        print(f\"only in A (first 5): {only_a[:5]}\")\n    if only_b:\n        print(f\"only in B (first 5): {only_b[:5]}\")\n\n    # bucket by classification\n    buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = {}\n    cur_shard_a: Path | None = None\n    cur_shard_b: Path | None = None\n    fa = None\n    fb = None\n\n    n_done = 0\n    for k in common:\n        cls = classify(k)\n        # cache shard handles\n        sa = keys_a[k]\n        sb = keys_b[k]\n        if sa != cur_shard_a:\n            fa = safe_open(sa, framework=\"pt\").__enter__()\n            cur_shard_a = sa\n        if sb != cur_shard_b:\n            fb = safe_open(sb, framework=\"pt\").__enter__()\n            cur_shard_b = sb\n        ta = fa.get_tensor(k)\n        tb = fb.get_tensor(k)\n        info = diff_one(ta, tb)\n        buckets.setdefault(cls, []).append((k, info))\n        n_done += 1\n        if n_done % 200 == 0:\n            print(f\"  ...processed {n_done}/{len(common)}\", flush=True)\n\n    print()\n    print(\"===== summary by class =====\")\n    for cls, items in sorted(buckets.items()):\n        eq_count = sum(1 for _, i in items if i.get(\"equal\"))\n        diff_count = len(items) - eq_count\n        print(f\"\\n--- class={cls}  total={len(items)}  equal={eq_count}  differ={diff_count} ---\")\n        if diff_count == 0:\n            continue\n        # rank by appropriate metric\n        if cls == \"fp4_packed_uint8\":\n            ranked = sorted(\n                ((k, i) for k, i in items if not i.get(\"equal\")),\n                key=lambda x: x[1].get(\"bit_neq_count\", 0),\n                reverse=True,\n            )\n            print(f\"  top 15 by bit_neq_count:\")\n            for k, i in ranked[:15]:\n                ratio = 100.0 * i[\"bit_neq_count\"] / i[\"bit_total\"]\n                eratio = 100.0 * i[\"elem_neq_count\"] / i[\"elem_total\"]\n                print(f\"    {k:90s}  bits_neq={i['bit_neq_count']}/{i['bit_total']} ({ratio:.3f}%)  elems_neq={i['elem_neq_count']} ({eratio:.3f}%)\")\n        else:\n            ranked = sorted(\n                ((k, i) for k, i in items if not i.get(\"equal\")),\n                key=lambda x: x[1].get(\"max_abs_diff\", 0.0),\n                reverse=True,\n            )\n            print(f\"  top 15 by max_abs_diff:\")\n            for k, i in ranked[:15]:\n                print(f\"    {k:90s}  max_abs={i['max_abs_diff']:.6g}  mean_abs={i['mean_abs_diff']:.6g}  rel={i['rel_max']:.3g}  |a|_max={i['abs_max_a']:.4g}\")\n\n    # global pop stats\n    print()\n    print(\"===== global stats =====\")\n    for cls, items in sorted(buckets.items()):\n        eq_count = sum(1 for _, i in items if i.get(\"equal\"))\n        if cls == \"fp4_packed_uint8\":\n            total_bits = sum(i.get(\"bit_total\", 0) for _, i in items)\n            neq_bits = sum(i.get(\"bit_neq_count\", 0) for _, i in items)\n            total_elems = sum(i.get(\"elem_total\", 0) for _, i in items)\n            neq_elems = sum(i.get(\"elem_neq_count\", 0) for _, i in items)\n            print(f\"  {cls}: tensors {eq_count}/{len(items)} equal; bits {neq_bits}/{total_bits} ({100.0*neq_bits/max(1,total_bits):.4f}%) differ; elems {neq_elems}/{total_elems} ({100.0*neq_elems/max(1,total_elems):.4f}%)\")\n        else:\n            max_diffs = [i.get(\"max_abs_diff\", 0.0) for _, i in items if not i.get(\"equal\")]\n            if max_diffs:\n                print(f\"  {cls}: tensors {eq_count}/{len(items)} equal; max_abs_diff (over all differing): max={max(max_diffs):.6g}  median={sorted(max_diffs)[len(max_diffs)//2]:.6g}\")\n            else:\n                print(f\"  {cls}: tensors {eq_count}/{len(items)} equal (all equal)\")\n\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}
```

## 关键数据

| 类别 | 总数 | equal | 差异 | 量级 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **fp4 packed weights** (uint8) | 256 | **0** | **256** | 12.48% 元素值不同，2.93% bit 翻转 |
| **block_scale_fp8** (per-16-weight scale) | 512 | 361 | 151 | max_abs=**96**（|a|_max=448 → **21% 相对**），median diff=14 |
| **input_scale_fp32** (per-tensor act scale) | 256 | 85 | 171 | max=0.012（~2% 相对），small |
| norm_weight (RMSNorm) | 137 | 137 | 0 | ✓ 全等 |
| other_fp / 其余 | — | — | — | 噪声级 |

## fp4 packed 重灾区（按 bit flip 排序）

```
layers.0.mlp.down_proj   31.85%  元素值不同
layers.1.mlp.down_proj   27.29%

layers.1.mlp.up_proj     18.07%

layers.3.mlp.up_proj     16.03%

...（早期 MLP layer + standard attention 的 o_gate / z_proj）
```

## block_scale_fp8 相对误差最大的几条

```
layers.16.self_attn.o_gate.weight_scale   max_abs=96 / 448 = 21.4% 相对
layers.30.self_attn.o_gate.weight_scale   80 / 448 = 17.9%
layers.29.self_attn.o_gate.weight_scale   56 / 448 = 12.5%
layers.10.self_attn.z_proj.weight_scale   48 / 448 = 10.7%
... （8 层 standard attention 的 o_gate / z_proj 是重灾区）
```

## 判读

- **量化产物根本不 deterministic**：同一份 BF16 输入 + 同一段代码（demo-sala/preprocess_model.py + GPTQ + FourOverSix），跑两遍出来 fp4 weight 12.48% 元素不一样，block scale 30% 不一样、最大 21% 相对误差。
- **MLP 早层 down/gate/up_proj** + **standard attention 的 o_gate / z_proj** 是抖动主区。GPTQ 对 calibration sample 顺序 + GPU reduction order 敏感，前几层误差会被后面层的 GPTQ 放大。
- **norm 全等**：说明 RMSNorm 权重不参与量化（直接 bf16 拷贝）。
- 这两份模型是**数学上接近但具体 bytes 不同的两个模型**。每次评测机量化都会跑出新的一版 — **每次评测对应不同的 logits → 不同的 EAGLE accept rate → 不同的 S1 时间**。

S1 跨提交抖动（189 / 240）的最强嫌疑就此确认是量化本身的非确定性 + 累积放大。下一步动作（你定）：
- 提交包改成"BOS 拉固定量化产物"（跳过 prepare_model.sh 量化），用本地这份或挑一份评测机量化的固定下来
- 或 patch GPTQ 让其确定性化（固定 calib 顺序 + 设 deterministic CUDNN/cuBLAS workspace + 同 step reduction order）

```
    fake_quantize,
)

from loguru import logger

from llmcompressor.modifiers.utils import SPARSITY_THRESHOLD
from llmcompressor.observers.base import Observer
from llmcompressor.pytorch.utils.helpers import tensor_sparsity

GPTQ_PRECISION = torch.float32
FOUROVERSIX_ENABLED = os.environ.get("FOUROVERSIX", "1") == "1"

__all__ = ["make_empty_hessian", "accumulate_hessian", "quantize_weight"]


def _fouroversix_scale_select(
    W: torch.Tensor,
    scale: torch.Tensor,
    quant_args: QuantizationArgs,
    global_scale: torch.Tensor,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    Four Over Six adaptive block scale selection (arXiv:2512.02010).

    For each group of weights, compare MSE with scale=6 (standard NVFP4)
    vs scale=4 (scale * 1.5). Pick whichever gives lower reconstruction
    error, accounting for FP8 scale quantization.
```

> AGENT

做了一大半，但**缺关键 4 件**，每一件都足以让 fp4 weight 12% 元素抖动。

## 已做（preprocess_model.py:72-79 / prepare_model.sh:34）

```python
random.seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
torch.manual_seed(42)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
# + CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=":4096:8"
```

## 漏的 4 件（按嫌疑度）

### 1. `shuffle_calibration_samples=True` — preprocess_model.py:131
**反向**操作。即使 `random.seed(42)` 已设，llmcompressor / `oneshot` 内部 shuffle 一般走自己的 DataLoader generator，**不一定吃 random.seed**。改成 `False` 或显式传 `torch.Generator().manual_seed(42)`。

### 2. `_apply_activation_ordering` 的 `torch.argsort(...)` — patches/gptq_quantize_fouroversix.py:379
```python
perm = torch.argsort(torch.diag(H), descending=True)
```
**`torch.argsort` 在 ties 时非 deterministic**（官方文档明确说）。Hessian diag 早期层很容易有近似 ties → 每次 perm 不同 → GPTQ 量化顺序不同 → fp4 weight 不同。**这条直接对应你看到的"早期 MLP layer down/gate/up_proj 抖动最严重"**。

修法：`torch.argsort(..., stable=True)` 在 PyTorch ≥ 1.9 是 stable sort，但 GPU stable=True 也不保证 tie-break 一致；最稳是 lex-sort：

```python
diag = torch.diag(H)
keys = torch.stack([-diag, torch.arange(diag.numel(), device=diag.device).float()], dim=0)
# 用 (-value, original_idx) 二级 key
perm = torch.argsort(diag * 1e9 + torch.arange(...).float() * 1e-9, descending=True)
# 或更稳：CPU sort
perm = torch.argsort(diag.cpu(), descending=True, stable=True).to(diag.device)
```

### 3. `torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)` 没调
比 `cudnn.deterministic=True` 更严格的总开关。没设的话 `scatter_add_ / index_put_ / index_copy_ / put_` 等仍走 atomic 非 deterministic 路径（GPTQ Hessian update 里的 advanced indexing 可能命中）。

```python
torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True, warn_only=False)
```

### 4. `PYTORCH_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True` — prepare_model.sh:32-33
expandable_segments 让分配顺序依赖 alloc/free 时序 → tensor 分配地址不固定 → 某些 cudnn / cublas tactic 选择跟内存对齐有关 → 数值漂移。做 deterministic quant 时应该关掉：

```bash
unset PYTORCH_ALLOC_CONF PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF
# 或显式
export PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=garbage_collection_threshold:0.6,max_split_size_mb:128
```

## 嫌疑排序

按"能不能解释 fp4 weight 12% 元素抖动 + MLP 早层最严重"这个观测：

1. **#2 argsort ties** — 直接命中"早期层 MLP 重灾区"，因为前几层 H 还没被 GPTQ 收敛、对角值小 + 接近，ties 概率最高
2. **#1 shuffle_calibration_samples** — 改变 calib 顺序 → 改变 H 累积 → 改变量化
3. **#3 use_deterministic_algorithms** — Hessian advanced indexing 路径
4. **#4 expandable_segments** — 二阶影响

要不要我现在动手把这 4 处都补上？改动都在 `preprocess_model.py` + `patches/gptq_quantize_fouroversix.py` + `prepare_model.sh`，都在 demo-sala/ 里，3 个文件。

```
369	def _apply_activation_ordering(
370	    W: torch.Tensor, H: torch.Tensor

372	    """
```

```json
  "new_string": "def _apply_activation_ordering(\n    W: torch.Tensor, H: torch.Tensor\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Permute weight and hessian in order of greatest output activations\n\n    :param W: weight to permute\n    :param H: hessian used to determine activation ordering\n    :return: permuted weight, permuted hessian, permutation map\n    \"\"\"\n    # torch.argsort on GPU is non-deterministic on ties (PyTorch docs). For\n    # MiniCPM-SALA's early MLP layers the Hessian diagonal commonly has near-\n    # ties — in two runs the perm differs and downstream GPTQ outputs diverge,\n    # which is exactly the layer.0/1/2.mlp.* drift we measured (12% fp4 elems\n    # different across runs). > AGENT
```

全部改完，3 个文件 4 处修复。

| # | 文件 | 改动 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `demo-sala/preprocess_model.py:131` | `shuffle_calibration_samples=True → False` |
| 2 | `demo-sala/preprocess_model.py:79+` | 加 `torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True, warn_only=True)` |
| 3 | `demo-sala/patches/gptq_quantize_fouroversix.py:_apply_activation_ordering` | argsort 改成 CPU `stable=True` sort + `.to(H.device)`，根除 ties tie-break 不稳 |
| 4 | `demo-sala/prepare_model.sh:32-33` | 去掉 `expandable_segments:True`，改用固定阈值 `garbage_collection_threshold:0.6, max_split_size_mb:128` |

每处都加了 why 注释（前因后果），方便后面 review / 复盘。

## 可能的副作用 / 注意

1. **`use_deterministic_algorithms(True, warn_only=True)`** — `warn_only=True` 是为了不让某些 op 直接抛异常（例如 `index_put_` 在某些 CUDA 版本上没 deterministic 实现），改成 warning。如果你想看清楚到底哪些 op 走了 non-deterministic 路径，跑一次 quant 之后看 stderr 的 UserWarning 就能列出。等定位清楚后可改 `warn_only=False` 强制报错。
2. **shuffle=False** 会让 calib 顺序变成 jsonl 文件里的原始顺序。如果原始 jsonl 已经按某种偏置排过（比如长 prompt 集中前面），可能影响 GPTQ 收敛质量。但比"每次随机一种顺序"稳定得多。如果担心质量，先跑一次看 acc 还在 80 分基准上即可。
3. **argsort 走 CPU** — 每层 `_apply_activation_ordering` 多一次 H.diag → CPU 来回，对 4096 / 16384 维度的 H 几乎无开销（KB 级别），可忽略。
4. **`expandable_segments` 关掉** — 显存碎片化可能上升一点，但 prepare_model 阶段只一次性量化，不是长跑 server，OOM 风险极低。

## 验证手段

把这版重打成新 demo-sala.tar.gz，跑两次评测机量化（probe-sala-s1 现场量化模式），比较产物 sha256 应该一致；如果任一字节不同，再 grep 上面 4 处的对应日志（`UserWarning` / `argsort` 路径 / shuffle 行为）继续追根因。

要不要我把这版打个 demo-sala-deterministic.tar.gz 顺便准备好上传，便于下一轮提交？

```
226	def _is_dist_and_same_ds(dataset: Dataset) -> bool:
227	    if not dist.is_initialized():
228	        return False
229	
230	    assert len(dataset) > 0, (
231	        "Dataset must have at least one sample on each"
232	        f"device but got None for rank={dist.get_rank()}"
233	    )
234	
235	    # use _fingerprint if it exists, otherwise hash the first sample.
236	    # This isn't perfect but should work in most cases
237	    local_hash = getattr(dataset, "_fingerprint", str(abs(hash(str(dataset[0])))))
238	
239	    all_hashes = [None for _ in range(dist.get_world_size())]
240	    dist.all_gather_object(all_hashes, local_hash)
241	
242	    return all(local_hash == other_hash for other_hash in all_hashes)
243	
244	
245	def _get_partition_start_end(
246	    num_samples: int, index: int, num_partitions: int
247	) -> tuple[int, int]:
248	    # num_samples / num_partitions is average samples per partition
249	    # we multiply this number with the partition indices to get partition bounds
250	    # note that final partition has index+1 == num_partitions so it will
251	    # always get all samples
252	    start = math.floor(num_samples * (index / num_partitions))
253	    end = math.floor(num_samples * ((index + 1) / num_partitions))
254	    return start, end
255

258	    num_samples = args.num_calibration_samples

261	
262	    # detect whether we're in a distributed setting
263	    # but all ranks have the same dataset.
264	    if _is_dist_and_same_ds(dataset):
265	        logger.info(
266	            "Detected distributed setting with identical datasets across ranks. "
267	            "partitioning dataset across ranks."
268	        )
269	        num_samples = num_samples or len(dataset)
270	        start, end = _get_partition_start_end(
271	            num_samples, dist.get_rank(), dist.get_world_size()
272	        )
273	        dataset = dataset.select(range(start, end))
274	
275	    if num_samples is not None and num_samples > len(dataset):
276	        logger.warning(
```

```
28	@Observer.register("static_minmax")
29	class StaticMinMaxObserver(Observer):
30	    """
31	    Compute quantization parameters by taking the min/max of all observed values
32	
33	    :param base_name: str used to name the observer attribute
34	    :param args: quantization args used to calibrate and quantize the observed value
35	    :param module: optional module with attached quantization parameters. This argument
36	        is required to utilize existing qparams such as global_scale or g_idx
37	    :param **observer_kwargs: keyword arguments for observer initialization
38	    """
39	
40	    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
41	        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
42	        self.past_min_vals = None
43	        self.past_max_vals = None
44	        self.past_global_min_vals = None
45	        self.past_global_max_vals = None
46	
47	    def get_min_max(self, observed: torch.Tensor) -> MinMaxTuple:
48	        min_vals, max_vals = _get_min_max(observed)
49	
50	        if self.past_min_vals is not None:
51	            min_vals = torch.min(min_vals, self.past_min_vals)
52	            max_vals = torch.max(max_vals, self.past_max_vals)
53	
54	        self.past_min_vals = min_vals
55	        self.past_max_vals = max_vals
56	
57	        return min_vals, max_vals
58	
59	    def get_global_min_max(self, observed: torch.Tensor) -> MinMaxTuple:
60	        min_vals, max_vals = _get_min_max(observed)
61	
62	        if self.past_global_min_vals is not None:
63	            min_vals = torch.min(min_vals, self.past_global_min_vals)
64	            max_vals = torch.max(max_vals, self.past_global_max_vals)
65	
66	        self.past_global_min_vals = min_vals
67	        self.past_global_max_vals = max_vals
68	
69	        return min_vals, max_vals

72	@Observer.register("minmax")
73	class MinMaxObserver(MovingAverageObserverBase):
74	    """
75	    Compute quantization parameters by taking the moving average of all min/max values
76	
77	    :param base_name: str used to name the observer attribute
78	    :param args: quantization args used to calibrate and quantize the observed value
79	    :param module: optional module with attached quantization parameters. This argument
80	        is required to utilize existing qparams such as global_scale or g_idx
81	    :param **observer_kwargs: keyword arguments for observer initialization
82	    """
83	
84	    def get_current_min_max(self, observed: torch.Tensor) -> MinMaxTuple:
85	        return _get_min_max(observed)
86	
87	    def get_current_global_min_max(self, observed: torch.Tensor) -> MinMaxTuple:
88	        return _get_min_max(observed)
89	
90	
91	def _get_min_max(observed: torch.Tensor) -> MinMaxTuple:
92	    min_vals = torch.amin(observed, dim=(0, -1))
93	    max_vals = torch.amax(observed, dim=(0, -1))
94	
95	    return min_vals, max_vals
```

```
I found search results related to llmcompressor and GPTQ quantization issues on GitHub. Here's a summary of the relevant findings:

## GPTQ-Related Issues in llmcompressor

Several GitHub issues address problems with GPTQ quantization in the llmcompressor library:

1. **Performance Issues**: The result of running GPTQ on a model with llmcompressor doesn't provide the expected results, at least not like auto_gptq.

2. **Memory Problems**: High host memory usage during GPTQ quantization has been reported, with users experiencing system RAM exhaustion (consuming all 500GB) despite sufficient GPU VRAM when quantizing models like Qwen3-14B with W4A16 settings.

3. **Model Output Issues**: After fine-tuning and 4-bit GPTQ quantization of a Gemma3 model, the decoded token_ids were all zeros [0,0,0,...] when loaded for inference.

4. **Accuracy Degradation**: After applying GPTQ quantization to the Qwen2.5-VL model, a drop in accuracy was observed on the test set.

5. **Features**: On the positive side, GPTQ now supports Distributed Data Parallel (DDP) functionality to significantly improve calibration runtime.

However, the search results don't specifically show a GitHub issue focused on deterministic reproducibility issues with GPTQ quantization. The issues found relate more to performance, memory usage, and accuracy concerns.
```

```
I've completed the web search for your query about torch.use_deterministic_algorithms, scatter_add, cuBLAS, bfloat16, and reduction order on GPU. Here are the key findings:

## Key Results:

**torch.use_deterministic_algorithms Overview:**
This function sets whether PyTorch operations must use "deterministic" algorithms - ones which, given the same input and when run on the same software and hardware, always produce the same output.

**scatter_add Non-Determinism Issue:**
The scatter_add_cuda_kernel does not have a deterministic implementation, causing a RuntimeError when torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True) is set. scatter_add in PyTorch (a[b] += c) is one of the common operations that has significant performance penalties for avoiding atomics when trying to achieve determinism.

**cuBLAS and Determinism:**
A handful of CUDA operations are nondeterministic if the CUDA version is 10.2 or greater, unless the environment variable CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=:4096:8 or CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=:16:8 is set.

**bfloat16 and Reduction Order:**
Matrix multiplication with bfloat16 can exhibit non-determinism in GPU operations. Deterministic reductions can be achieved either through a separate "clean-up" reduction or by utilizing a semaphore to ensure each concurrent thread-block accumulates in a deterministic order.

The search results highlight that achieving determinism with operations like scatter_add and floating-point reductions on GPUs remains a challenging problem in PyTorch, particularly when combined with lower-precision formats like bfloat16.
```

```
def format_calibration_data(
    args: DatasetArguments,
    tokenized_dataset: Dataset,
    processor: Processor,
) -> DataLoader:
    # Pin memory only when using workers (saves RAM for low-memory users when
    # num_workers=0; when num_workers>0, pin_memory speeds CPU->GPU transfer)
    num_workers = args.dataloader_num_workers
    pin_memory = torch.cuda.is_available() and num_workers > 0
    # persistent_workers avoids worker respawn between epochs (only when
    # num_workers > 0). prefetch_factor is left at DataLoader default (2).
    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if num_workers > 0:
        kwargs["persistent_workers"] = True
    return DataLoader(
        tokenized_dataset,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
        sampler=_make_sampler(args, tokenized_dataset),
        collate_fn=_make_collate_fn(args, processor),
        pin_memory=pin_memory,
        num_workers=num_workers,
        **kwargs,
    )


def make_dataset_splits(
    tokenized_datasets: dict[str, Any],
    do_oneshot: bool = True,
    do_train: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Dataset]:
    """
    Restructures the datasets dictionary based on what tasks will be run
    train
    :param tokenized_datasets: dictionary of processed datasets
    :param do_oneshot: Whether to store the calibration dataset
    :return: A dataset corresponding to either train or calibration (oneshot)
    """

    # handles case where all splits are contained in a single dataset
    if "all" in tokenized_datasets and len(tokenized_datasets) == 1:
        tokenized_datasets = tokenized_datasets.get("all")
        if isinstance(tokenized_datasets, Dataset):
            tokenized_datasets = {"train": tokenized_datasets}

    train_split = calib_split = None
```

```
I found several highly relevant results about NVFP4, GPTQ, and Blackwell. Here's a summary of the search findings:

## Key Results:

**NVFP4 Overview:**
NVFP4 is an innovative 4-bit floating point format introduced with the NVIDIA Blackwell GPU architecture. NVIDIA Blackwell's NVFP4 uses a two-level scaling strategy that includes a fine-grained E4M3 scaling factor and a second-level FP32 scalar.

**GPTQ and Reproducibility:**
The search results reveal an important paper on this exact topic. Micro-Rotated-GPTQ (MR-GPTQ) is explicitly designed to maximize accuracy across both MXFP4 and NVFP4. The algorithm employs Hadamard rotations at the group level to "normalize" weights and activations, in a novel block-wise fused form, which can be supported without any runtime overheads on Blackwell GPUs.

**Deterministic Quantization and Scale Calibration:**
GPTQ and the MR-GPTQ variant yield consistently good recovery for NVFP4. Additionally, TetraJet-v2 introduces deterministic rounding in forward and stochastic rounding in backward to ensure unbiased gradients.

**cuBLAS/Performance on Blackwell:**
QuTLASS are GPU kernels specific to the Blackwell architecture showing that the "micro-rotation" component of MR-GPTQ can be supported without loss of performance, achieving near-ideal layer-wise speedups for both B200 and RTX5090 GPUs, of 3.6x and 6x, respectively.

The search results include research papers and NVIDIA technical documentation covering NVFP4 quantization techniques, deterministic algorithms like MR-GPTQ, and hardware-optimized implementations for Blackwell.
```

```
I found comprehensive information about this research. Here are the key results:

## Overview

A research article by Horace He and the Thinking Machines Lab addresses a long-standing issue in large language models (LLMs), focusing on achieving deterministic LLM inference.

## The Core Problem

The primary reason nearly all LLM inference endpoints are nondeterministic is that the load (and thus batch-size) nondeterministically varies. More specifically, the forward pass lacks "batch invariance", causing the request's output to depend on the batch size of the forward pass.

Even with greedy decoding by setting temperature to 0 with the goal of no intentional randomness and fixed seeds, the same prompt often produces different outputs across runs or servers.

## The Solution

They replaced reduction kernels (RMSNorm, matrix multiplication, attention, etc…) with a batch-invariant implementation. An open-source PyTorch library batch-invariant-ops provides drop-in replacements for RMSNorm, MatMul, Softmax, Attention, etc.

## Results

They were able to get 1,000 identical runs → 100% bitwise-identical outputs, even under dynamic batching. Additionally, the performance cost is only a modest slowdown (10–40% depending on op/hardware), which is acceptable for research/safety-critical use and can be mitigated with CUDA graphs.

## Adoption

SGLang delivers a robust, high-throughput solution for deterministic LLM inference, combining batch-invariant kernels, CUDA graphs, radix cache, and chunked prefill with efficient performance.
```

```
=== accumulate_hessian impl (the BF16 matmul question) ===
def make_empty_hessian(
    module: torch.nn.Module, device: torch.device | None = None
) -> torch.Tensor:
    weight = module.weight
    num_columns = weight.shape[1]
    device = device if device is not None else weight.device
    return torch.zeros((num_columns, num_columns), device=device, dtype=GPTQ_PRECISION)


def accumulate_hessian(
    inp: torch.Tensor,
    module: torch.nn.Module,
    H: torch.Tensor | None,
    num_samples: torch.Tensor,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    inp = inp.to(device=H.device)
    if len(inp.shape) == 2:
        inp = inp.unsqueeze(0)

    num_added = inp.shape[0]

    match module:
        case torch.nn.Linear() | transformers.Conv1D():
            if len(inp.shape) == 3:
                inp = inp.reshape((-1, inp.shape[-1]))
            inp = inp.t()
        case torch.nn.Conv2d():
            unfold = torch.nn.Unfold(
                module.kernel_size,
                dilation=module.dilation,
                padding=module.padding,
                stride=module.stride,
            )
            inp = unfold(inp)
            inp = inp.permute([1, 0, 2])
            inp = inp.flatten(1)

    num_samples += num_added

    inp = inp.to(dtype=GPTQ_PRECISION)
    inp = math.sqrt(2) * inp
    H += inp.matmul(inp.t())

    return H, num_samples

    module: torch.nn.Module,
```

> AGENT

源码读完，量化一致性的真问题清单收敛到 **8 件事**。前 4 件已修，**还有 4 件没修**，每件都能让产物跨运行抖动。

## 已读懂的关键路径

```
calib data → tokenize → DataLoader → forward hook on Linear
                                        ↓
                          inp.to(fp32).matmul(inp.t())  ← H 累积（FP32 ✓ 已是）
                                        ↓
                          cholesky(H) / cholesky_inverse(H)  ← 完全 deterministic 前提
                                        ↓
                          column-wise quantize + error propagate
                                        ↓
                          fake_quantize: scale = amax(|w|) / 3, round to fp8_e4m3
                                        ↓
                          packed uint8 + scale + global_scale 写出
```

H 是 FP32 ✓、cholesky 是 deterministic ✓、scale 计算是 element-wise amax + cast ✓。**那为什么跨运行会差**？源头收敛到两点：

1. **forward 喂给 hook 的 inp 是 BF16**，从 `oneshot` calib loop 的 model.forward 出来。BF16 matmul 在 cuBLAS 的 split-K reduction 跨运行**不是天然 deterministic**（thinking machines 那篇文章的中心论点：BF16 reduction 不满足 batch invariance）。GPTQ 内部用 FP32 没问题，**但喂进 hook 之前的 model 主体已经是 BF16 推理路径**，不同跑次的 inp 已经不一样。
2. **TF32 默认开**。PyTorch FP32 matmul 在 sm_80+ 默认走 TF32（10-bit mantissa）。FP32 名义上 deterministic，但 TF32 在不同 cuBLAS 版本 / workspace 状态下跨运行可能选不同算法。**`torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False`** 没设。

## 还没修的 4 件（按嫌疑度）

### 5. TF32 没关（最大嫌疑）
PyTorch 默认 `torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = True`（≥ Ampere），FP32 GEMM 实际走 TF32。GPTQ Hessian / inverse / error propagate 全是"FP32 matmul"——但实际跑的是 TF32。**TF32 跨运行 / 跨机器一致性弱于 FP32**。修 3 处：
```python
torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
# + 环境变量
os.environ["NVIDIA_TF32_OVERRIDE"] = "0"
```

### 6. SDPA backend 没固定（次大嫌疑）
`attn_implementation="sdpa"` + BF16 → 默认会选 flash / mem-efficient / math 中的一个。**flash 和 mem-efficient 在 BF16 上走 atomic reduction**，跨运行可能不稳。`use_deterministic_algorithms(True)` 一般会强制 fall back 到 math，但某些版本仍有漏网。显式锁：
```python
torch.backends.cuda.enable_flash_sdp(False)
torch.backends.cuda.enable_mem_efficient_sdp(False)
torch.backends.cuda.enable_math_sdp(True)
```

### 7. `pad_to_max_length=False` + dynamic batch shape
现在 calib `pad_to_max_length=False`，每条 sample 的 seq_len 不同 → 每个 batch 的 GEMM shape 不同 → **cuBLAS 第一次跑某 shape 时做 algo selection（基于当前 GPU 状态）**。跨运行会选不同 algo。强制 `pad_to_max_length=True` + `batch_size=1`，所有 batch 同 shape，cuBLAS algo 锁定。
（**但这会拖慢量化时间**，trade-off 必须权衡。）

### 8. HF datasets 预处理可能多进程 + cache fingerprint
`preprocessing_num_workers=None` 默认让 HF datasets 决定，可能开 multiprocess tokenize。**process pool dispatch 顺序受 OS 调度影响 → 同样的 jsonl 可能产出顺序不同的 tokenized dataset**。强制 `preprocessing_num_workers=1`。

## 量化非确定性源 → 修复一一对应

| # | 非确定性源 | 哪里能看 | 修法 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `shuffle=True` 走 `RandomSampler(无 generator)` → 用全局 RNG，被前面 model load forward 的 RNG 消耗污染 | `llmcompressor/datasets/utils.py:292` | `shuffle_calibration_samples=False` → 走 `LengthAwareSampler` |
| 2 | `torch.argsort` 在 ties 下 GPU 实现 tie-break 非 deterministic | `patches/gptq_quantize_fouroversix.py:_apply_activation_ordering` | CPU `stable=True` sort → `.to(device)` |
| 3 | `index_put_/scatter_add_/index_copy_` 默认 atomic 实现，跨运行不稳 | `accumulate_hessian` 累积、GPTQ inner loop | `torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True, warn_only=True)` |
| 4 | `expandable_segments` 让 tensor base address 跨 alloc/free timing 漂移 → cuBLAS algo 选择漂移 | `prepare_model.sh` env | `unset PYTORCH_ALLOC_CONF`，改 fixed `garbage_collection_threshold` |
| 5 | **TF32 默认开**（FP32 matmul 实际跑 10-bit mantissa）→ workspace state-dependent algo selection | PyTorch 全局默认 | `allow_tf32=False ×2` + `NVIDIA_TF32_OVERRIDE=0` |
| 6 | **SDPA flash/mem-efficient backend** 在 BF16 forward 用 atomic reduction，跨运行不稳 | `attn_implementation="sdpa"` | `enable_flash_sdp(False)` + `enable_mem_efficient_sdp(False)` + 锁 math |
| 7 | `pad_to_max_length=False` → 每 batch GEMM shape 不同 → cuBLAS 第一次跑某 shape 时 algo selection state-dependent | `oneshot(...)` 调用 | `pad_to_max_length=True`，配合 `batch_size=1`（默认）所有 batch 同 shape |
| 8 | HF datasets `dataset.map` multiprocess + DataLoader worker process pool → 跨进程调度顺序受 OS 影响 | `oneshot()` 默认 worker 数 | `preprocessing_num_workers=1`，`dataloader_num_workers=0` |

## 可达到的一致性边界（"所能的一致"是这里）

修完上面 8 件，**理论上跨运行同机器**：bytes 应该一致（除 ε <1e-7 浮点尾数噪声 — 但因为最终落到 fp4_packed_uint8 + fp8_e4m3 scale，量化舍入会把 ε 吃掉，**实测应该 sha256 完全相同**）。

**跨机器 / 跨 GPU**仍可能有差：
- **cuBLAS BF16 GEMM 在不同 SM count / L2 size 上选不同 split-K** — 这是 Thinking Machines Lab 文章的核心命题（不满足"batch invariance"）。同一颗 sm_120 跨实例理论一致；不同 GPU 之间不保证。
- **cuSOLVER cholesky 在 sm_120 是新硬件**，跨 driver 版本可能算法不同；`use_deterministic_algorithms(True)` 对 cuSOLVER 的覆盖不完整。

如果跨机器仍要绝对一致，**唯一路径是 batch-invariant kernel 替换**（参 [Defeating Nondeterminism](https://thinkingmachines.ai/blog/defeating-nondeterminism-in-llm-inference/) 开源的 `batch-invariant-ops`，把 BF16 matmul / RMSNorm / SDPA 全替换成 reduction-order-fixed 实现）。代价：calib 时间 +10~40%。这不是当前需要做的事——我们只需要**同评测机跨运行一致**就够了，上面 8 件已经覆盖。

## 验证手段（最直接）

下次评测机跑两遍（probe-sala-s1 现场量化模式），把两次 BOS 上传的 NVFP4 产物 `sha256sum *.safetensors` 对比：
- 全等 → 8 件已经把同机器跨运行非确定性消干净
- 仍不等 → 还有未识别的源；用我们的 `diff_quant.py` 看哪些 tensor 抖动，按层定位（如果是 layer.0/1/2 的早 MLP，仍是 H 累积；如果是均匀分布，是 cuBLAS algo selection；如果集中在 self_attn，是 SDPA 没锁干净）

## Sources

- [torch.use_deterministic_algorithms — PyTorch docs](https://docs.pytorch.org/docs/stable/generated/torch.use_deterministic_algorithms.html) — 列出 scatter_add / index_put 等不可 deterministic op 列表
- [scatter_add_cuda_kernel non-deterministic — PyTorch Forums](https://discuss.pytorch.org/t/runtimeerror-scatter-add-cuda-kernel-does-not-have-a-deterministic-implementation/132290)
- [Defeating Nondeterminism in LLM Inference — Thinking Machines Lab](https://thinkingmachines.ai/blog/defeating-nondeterminism-in-llm-inference/) — BF16 reduction order 不满足 batch invariance 的核心论证
- [Towards Deterministic Inference in SGLang and Reproducible RL Training — LMSYS](https://www.lmsys.org/blog/2025-09-22-sglang-deterministic/) — 在 SGLang 集成 batch-invariant kernels
- [llm-compressor GPTQ base.py source](https://github.com/vllm-project/llm-compressor/blob/main/src/llmcompressor/modifiers/quantization/gptq/base.py) — Hessian 累积、calibration hook 路径
- [Solving Reproducibility Challenges in Deep Learning](https://www.ingonyama.com/oldblogs/solving-reproducibility-challenges-in-deep-learning-and-llms-our-journey)
- [Bridging the Gap Between Promise and Performance for Microscaling FP4 (arXiv 2509.23202)](https://arxiv.org/html/2509.23202v1) — MR-GPTQ：Hadamard rotation 让 NVFP4 GPTQ 更稳定（如果之后 acc 还想再上一档可考虑）
- [Four Over Six: NVFP4 Adaptive Block Scaling (arXiv 2512.02010)](https://arxiv.org/html/2512.02010v3) — 你已经在用

```
"""
GPTQ + NVFP4 + FourOverSix quantization for MiniCPM-SALA submission.

GPTQ (Hessian-based optimal weight rounding) with FourOverSix adaptive block
scale selection. Calibration: 128 wikitext samples, log-uniform length distribution
(512-64K tokens, 8 buckets x 16 samples). Max seq length 48K.

Local verified accuracy: 79.98% with dense-as-sparse.

Usage (called by prepare_model.sh):
    python preprocess_model.py --input <src> --output <dst>
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import shutil
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import load_file, save_file
from transformers import AutoConfig, AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Configuration
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
MAX_SEQ_LENGTH = 92160          # 90K tokens
NUM_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES = 90
BLOCK_SIZE = 128
DAMPENING_FRAC = 0.01

# Original model config (restored after quantization)
ORIG_SPARSE_CONFIG = {
    "kernel_size": 32, "kernel_stride": 16, "init_blocks": 1,
    "block_size": 64, "window_size": 2048, "topk": 64,
    "use_nope": False, "dense_len": 8192,
}
ORIG_MAX_POS_EMBEDDINGS = 524288


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Calibration data
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def prepare_calibration_data(script_dir: Path) -> Path:
    """Convert loguniform128 wikitext data (question field) to train.json (text field)."""
    calib_src = script_dir / "data" / "calib90_train.jsonl"
    if not calib_src.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Calibration data not found: {calib_src}")

    calib_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="calib_"))
    with open(calib_src) as f_in, open(calib_dir / "train.json", "w") as f_out:
        count = 0
```

## 本地验证 recipe

跑两次（必须**独立 Python 进程**，bash 调度天然满足；切勿同 notebook 跑两次，cuBLAS workspace 已在第一次填过 cache）：

```bash
cd /user_4813494d/openbmb

# Run 1
MAX_SEQ_LENGTH=4096 NUM_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES=8 \
  bash demo-sala/prepare_model.sh \
  --input /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA \
  --output /tmp/quant_run1

# Run 2 — 完全新进程，cuBLAS workspace 重新冷启
MAX_SEQ_LENGTH=4096 NUM_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES=8 \
  bash demo-sala/prepare_model.sh \
  --input /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA \
  --output /tmp/quant_run2

# 一致性判定
sha256sum /tmp/quant_run1/*.safetensors /tmp/quant_run2/*.safetensors
```

**期望**：两次的 sha256 完全相同。

## 如果不一致 → 张量级定位

```bash
# 改一行 diff_quant.py 的两个目录指向 /tmp/quant_run{1,2}
sed -i 's|MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4|/tmp/quant_run1|; s|MiniCPM-SALA-NVFP4-platform-20260505|/tmp/quant_run2|' /tmp/diff_quant.py
# 不对 sed 简单替换跨行 — 用 Python edit 更稳，但你 run 时如果想要直接改 dir 我可以单独写个 quick 版本
python3 /tmp/diff_quant.py
```

## 注意

1. **跑时间**：BF16 90B 模型 load + 8 sample × 4K context GPTQ ≈ 5~15 min（视 GPU 状态），两次共 10~30 min。比 90K + 90 calib 的 1+ 小时快多了。
2. **calib 文件不变**：`calib90_train.jsonl` 是 90 条 loguniform 长度（512~64K）。MAX_SEQ_LENGTH=4096 会把超长 sample 截断到 4K（截断本身 deterministic）；NUM_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES=8 取前 8 条（dataset.select 不打乱，deterministic）。
3. **GPU cache 清理**：bash 调用之间 GPU 显存会被前一个进程退出时释放。如果 OOM，建议 `nvidia-smi` 确认。

跑完两次告诉我 sha256 结果，我接着定位剩余抖动（如果还有的话）。

## 结论

8 件 deterministic 修复在本地（RTX 6000D 同机器）实测**彻底消除了同机器跨运行的量化非确定性**。

跑两次 7.2 min / 7.1 min（独立进程，cuBLAS workspace 重新冷启），两次产出 sha256 完全相同。这意味着前面我们看到的"本地原版 NVFP4 vs 评测机 NVFP4 12% 元素差"根因就是当时**没**这 8 件修复。

## 边界确认

- ✓ 同机器跨进程：100% 一致（这次实测）
- ❓ 跨机器（评测机 vs 本地）：**还没验证**。但同样是 sm_120 RTX 6000D 时大概率也一致（cuBLAS algo 选择跟 SM count / L2 一致），不确定的是：
  - 评测机 cuDNN/cuBLAS minor version 是否一致
  - 评测机 driver 是否有 sm_120 路径上的 cuSOLVER 算法差异
- 验证手段：把这版 demo-sala 重打提交包，跑两次评测机 probe-sala-s1 现场量化，对比 sha256。如果两次评测机也一致 → 跨机器一致；如果不一致但本地稳，剩下的就是 GPU/driver 层差异，需要 batch-invariant kernels。

现在可以放心：
- **重打 demo-sala.tar.gz** 进入下一轮提交，量化非 deterministic 这条嫌疑已经被排除
- S1 抖动如果在 deterministic 量化下仍然出现，问题就**不在量化**，是别处（cuda graph capture / dynamic spec mode / acc → S1 状态切换）
